75	export function expectedPrices(storeValue: number, s: SellSettings = {}): ExpectedPrices {
76	  const pa = s.priceAdjustment ?? 1;              // NotInShop = 1f（ShoppingSettings.cs:11-14）
77	  let selling = storeValue;                        // :34915 calcForSelling = storeValue
78	  let buying = storeValue;                         // :34916 calcForBuying  = storeValue
79	  if (s.discountAvailable) {                       // :34917
80	    if (!s.buyOnce) {
81	      // :34921 calcForBuying = (int)((float)calcForBuying * 0.8f)
82	      // (int) 强转 = 向零截断；float 乘法以 f32 逐位对齐
83	      buying = Math.trunc(f32(f32(buying) * f32(0.8)));
84	    }
85	    if (s.isAShopItem) selling = buying;           // :34923-34926
86	  }
87	  if (s.buyOnce) {
88	    // :34930-34931 (int)Math.Round((float)x / PriceAdjustment)
89	    // Math.Round(double) 默认银行家舍入（.5 → 就近偶数）→ roundCS
90	    buying = roundCS(f32(f32(buying) / f32(pa)));
91	    selling = roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa)));
92	  } else {
93	    buying = roundCS(f32(f32(buying) * f32(pa)));  // :34935
94	    selling = roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa))); // :34936 快乐度作用于卖价（÷ 而非 ×）
95	  }
96	  if (s.buyOnce) {
97	    buying = Math.floor(buying / 5);               // :34940 calcForBuying /= 5L（long 除 = 取整）
98	    if (storeValue !== 0 && buying < 1) buying = 1; // :34941-34944
99	  }
100	  return { selling, buying, storeValue };
101	}

119	//   :34732  long num = calcForSelling / 5;                  ← 卖价 = 价值/5
120	//   :34733  if (num < 1) num = 1L;                          ← 每件最小 1 铜
121	//   :34737  long num2 = num;
122	//   :34738  num *= stack;                                   ← 堆叠按 stack 相加
123	//   :34739  amount = Main.shopSellbackHelper.GetAmount(item);
124	//   :34740  if (amount > 0)
125	//   :34742      num += (-num2 + calcForBuying) * Math.Min(amount, item.stack);
126	//           ← 店内刚买的按【买价】原额退（差额补齐），仅覆盖记账数量
127	//   :34745-34868 币面额换装入包（74 铂/73 金/72 银/71 铜）→ Game.gainCopper 消费
128	//   :34869-34876 装不下 → 全量回滚 → Game.npcShopSell 消费
129	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
130	export function computeSellValue(vid: number, stack: number, settings: SellSettings = {}): SellQuote {
131	  const prices = expectedPrices(itemStoreValue(vid), settings);
132	  if (prices.selling <= 0) {                       // :34719-34722
133	    return { sellable: false, copper: 0, perUnit: 0, prices };
134	  }
135	  let perUnit = Math.floor(prices.selling / 5);    // :34732 long 除 = 向下取整（非负）
136	  if (perUnit < 1) perUnit = 1;                    // :34733-34736
137	  let total = perUnit * stack;                     // :34737-34738
138	  const amount = settings.sellbackAmount ?? 0;     // :34739
139	  if (amount > 0) {                                // :34740
140	    total += (prices.buying - perUnit) * Math.min(amount, stack); // :34742
141	  }
142	  return { sellable: true, copper: total, perUnit, prices };
143	}

149	//   :195  return Sell;                              ← 店内非钱币 = 卖出
150	// Game/UI 的门禁消费此函数（钱币不进卖出，也不回落垃圾桶）。
151	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
152	export function canSellFromShopUi(vid: number): boolean {
153	  return !(vid >= 71 && vid <= 74);

```
	public void GetItemExpectedPrice(Item item, out long calcForSelling, out long calcForBuying)
	{
		if (item.shopSpecialCurrency != -1)
		{
			CustomCurrencyManager.GetPrices(item, out calcForSelling, out calcForBuying);
			return;
		}
		int storeValue = item.GetStoreValue();
		calcForSelling = storeValue;
		calcForBuying = storeValue;
		if (discountAvailable)
		{
			if (!item.buyOnce)
			{
				calcForBuying = (int)((float)calcForBuying * 0.8f);
			}
			if (item.isAShopItem)
			{
				calcForSelling = calcForBuying;
			}
		}
		if (item.buyOnce)
		{
			calcForBuying = (int)Math.Round((float)calcForBuying / currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment);
			calcForSelling = (int)Math.Round((float)calcForSelling / currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment);
		}
		else
		{
			calcForBuying = (int)Math.Round((float)calcForBuying * currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment);
			calcForSelling = (int)Math.Round((float)calcForSelling / currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment);
		}
		if (item.buyOnce)
		{
			calcForBuying /= 5L;
			if (storeValue != 0 && calcForBuying < 1)
			{
				calcForBuying = 1L;
			}
		}
	}
```

```
1061	  private placeHeld(i: number, where: SlotWhere): void {
1062	    if (!this.heldStack) return;

1064	      const inv = this.game.player.inv;
1065	      const arr = this.equipArray(where);
1066	      // 部位/配饰/染料/杂项分类校验（ItemSlot.PickItemMovementAction 同源判定）
1067	      if (where === 'armor' && !inv.armorAccepts(i, this.heldStack.id)) return;
1068	      if (where === 'dye' && !inv.dyeAccepts(this.heldStack.id)) return;
1069	      if (where === 'misc' && !inv.miscAccepts(i, this.heldStack.id)) return;
1070	      if (where === 'miscDye' && !inv.dyeAccepts(this.heldStack.id)) return;
1071	      const cur = arr[i];
1072	      if (!cur) {
1073	        arr[i] = this.heldStack;
1074	        this.heldStack = null;
1075	      } else if (cur.id === this.heldStack.id) {
1076	        const max = ITEM_DEFS[cur.id].maxStack;
1077	        const put = Math.min(max - cur.stack, this.heldStack.stack);
1078	        cur.stack += put;
1079	        this.heldStack.stack -= put;
1080	        if (this.heldStack.stack <= 0) this.heldStack = null;
1081	      } else {
1082	        // 交换前反向校验（取出的物品总是合法的，防御性保留）
1083	        if (where === 'armor' && !inv.armorAccepts(i, cur.id)) return;
1084	        arr[i] = this.heldStack;
1085	        this.heldStack = cur;
1086	      }
1087	      this.refreshAll();

1107	      } else {
1108	        inv.slots[i] = this.heldStack;
1109	        this.heldStack = cur;
1110	      }
1111	    } else {

1123	        if (this.heldStack.stack <= 0) this.heldStack = null;
1124	      } else {
1125	        chest.items[i] = this.heldStack;
1126	        this.heldStack = cur;
1127	      }
1128	    }
```

```
122	/** 铜币计价格式化(原版 tooltip:金/银/铜) */
123	export function formatCopper(v: number): string {
124	  const gold = Math.floor(v / 10000);
125	  const silver = Math.floor((v % 10000) / 100);
126	  const copper = v % 100;
127	  let s = '';
128	  if (gold) s += `${gold}金`;
129	  if (silver) s += `${silver}银`;
130	  if (copper || !s) s += `${copper}铜`;
131	  return s;
132	}
133	
134	export class NpcDialog {
135	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
136	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
137	
138	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onButton: (id: NpcButtonId) => void) {
139	    if (!document.getElementById('sw-npc-style')) {
140	      const style = document.createElement('style');
141	      style.id = 'sw-npc-style';
142	      style.textContent = CSS;
143	      document.head.appendChild(style);
144	    }
145	  }
146	
147	  /** 打开对话框:name=NPC 名,chat=台词,buttons=选项(原版 Lang.inter 标签) */
148	  open(name: string, chat: string, buttons: Array<{ id: NpcButtonId; label: string }>, portrait?: string | null) {
149	    this.close();
150	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
151	    panel.className = 'sw-npc-dialog';
152	    const btns = buttons.map((b) => `<button data-b="${b.id}">${b.label}</button>`).join('');
153	    // 头像(原版 Main.npcChatPortrait = Images/TownNPCs/Portraits/Portrait_<Name>，SetTalkNPC :4196;
154	    // 打开时 DoNPCPortraitHop 弹跳)——无头像类型(如骷髅商人外的野兽)原版也不显示
155	    const portraitHtml = portrait
156	      ? `<img class="sw-npc-portrait" src="${portrait}">`
157	      : '';
158	    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="sw-npc-body">${portraitHtml}<div class="sw-npc-main">`
159	      + `<div class="sw-npc-name">${name}</div><div class="sw-npc-chat">${chat}</div>`
160	      + `<div class="sw-npc-btns">${btns}</div></div></div>`;
161	    panel.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((el) => {
162	      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
163	        UISfx.click(); // 原版对话按钮 MenuTick(id 12)
164	        this.onButton((el as HTMLElement).dataset.b as NpcButtonId);
165	      });
166	    });
167	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
168	    this.panel = panel;
169	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onButton('close'); };
170	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
171	  }
172	
173	  close() {
174	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
175	    this.escHandler = null;
176	    this.panel?.remove();
177	    this.panel = null;
178	  }
179	
180	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
181	}
182	
183	export class NpcShop {
184	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
185	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
186	
187	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onBuy: (vanillaId: number) => void, private onClose: () => void) {}
188	
189	  open(title: string, items: ShopEntry[], copper: number, happinessPct?: number | null) {
190	    this.close();
191	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
192	    panel.className = 'sw-npc-shop';
193	    const rows = items.map((it) => `
194	      <div class="sw-shop-item${copper < it.price ? ' poor' : ''}" data-id="${it.vanillaId}" data-ok="${copper >= it.price ? 1 : 0}">
195	        ${it.iconUrl ? `<img src="${it.iconUrl}">` : '<span style="width:26px;height:26px;display:inline-block"></span>'}
196	        <span class="nm">${it.name}</span><span class="pr">${formatCopper(it.price)}</span>
197	      </div>`).join('');
198	    // 快乐度表情 + 价格百分比（Main.cs:41231-41243 商店标题旁 NPCHappiness 贴图 +
199	    //  priceAdjustment.ToString("P0")；DOM 版用表情档位代替贴图帧）
200	    const happy = happinessPct != null
201	      ? `<span class="sw-shop-happy ${happinessFace(happinessPct).cls}">${happinessFace(happinessPct).face} ${happinessPct}</span>`
202	      : '';
203	    panel.innerHTML = `
204	      <div class="sw-shop-title">${title}${happy}</div>
205	      <div class="sw-shop-coins">${formatCopper(copper)}</div>
206	      <div class="sw-shop-list">${rows}</div>
207	      <div class="sw-shop-foot"><button class="sw-set-back" style="background:#3a4680;color:#e8e8f4;border:1px solid #7d92d6;border-radius:4px;padding:5px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;">${Lang.inter(52)}</button></div>`;
208	    panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-shop-item').forEach((el) => {
209	      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
210	        if ((el as HTMLElement).dataset.ok !== '1') return;
211	        this.onBuy(Number((el as HTMLElement).dataset.id));
212	      });
213	    });
214	    (panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back') as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.onClose());
215	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
216	    this.panel = panel;
217	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onClose(); };
218	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
219	  }
220	
221	  close() {
222	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
223	    this.escHandler = null;
224	    this.panel?.remove();
225	    this.panel = null;
226	  }
227	
228	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
229	
230	  /** 原地升级某商品行图标(开店时 iconUrl 一次成型的 miss 冻结问题,2026-08-13
231	   *  枚举 review):懒加载晚到由 UI 侧轮询驱动补图。无该行/已关店静默 */
232	  setRowIcon(vanillaId: number, url: string): void {
233	    const row = this.panel?.querySelector(`.sw-shop-item[data-id="${vanillaId}"]`);
234	    if (!row) return;
235	    const img = row.querySelector('img');
236	    if (img) { if (img.getAttribute('src') !== url) img.src = url; return; }
237	    const ph = row.querySelector('span');
238	    const el = document.createElement('img');
239	    el.src = url;
240	    if (ph && ph.tagName === 'SPAN') ph.replaceWith(el); else row.insertBefore(el, row.firstChild);
241	  }
242	}
243	
244	/** NPC 快乐度详情浮层（ReportHappiness.Interact：NPCInteractions.cs:303-313）。
245	 *  原版点「快乐度」按钮后把 HappinessReport 整段写进 npcChatText + 头像弹跳；
246	 *  DOM 对话框台词区定高，此处等价为独立浮层逐条列出，并附价格倍率
247	 *  （Main.cs:41240 priceAdjustment.ToString("P0") 同口径）。 */
248	export class NpcHappinessPanel {
249	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
250	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
251	
252	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onClose: () => void) {}
253	
254	  open(info: HappinessInfo) {
255	    this.close();
256	    const face = happinessFace(info.priceMul);
257	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
258	    panel.className = 'sw-happy-panel';
259	    const rows = (info.lines.length ? info.lines : ['—'])
260	      .map((t) => `<div class="sw-happy-line">${t}</div>`).join('');
261	    panel.innerHTML = `
262	      <div class="sw-happy-head">
263	        <div class="sw-happy-title">${info.name}</div>
264	        <span class="sw-happy-price ${face.cls}">${face.face} ${happinessPct(info.priceMul)}</span>
265	      </div>
266	      <div class="sw-happy-list">${rows}</div>
267	      <div class="sw-happy-foot"><button class="sw-happy-close" style="background:#3a4680;color:#e8e8f4;border:1px solid #7d92d6;border-radius:4px;padding:5px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;">${Lang.inter(52)}</button></div>`;
268	    (panel.querySelector('.sw-happy-close') as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.close());
269	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
270	    this.panel = panel;
271	    // 捕获阶段拦截 Esc：只关浮层，不连带关掉背后的对话框
272	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
273	      if (e.code === 'Escape') { e.stopImmediatePropagation(); this.close(); }
274	    };
275	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);
276	  }
277	
278	  close() {
279	    const had = this.panel !== null;   // 重开（open 首行 close）不触发回调
280	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);
281	    this.escHandler = null;
282	    this.panel?.remove();
283	    this.panel = null;
284	    if (had) this.onClose();
285	  }
286	
287	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
288	}
289	

```

```
	public static bool DestroyChest(int X, int Y)
	{
		if (!_chestsByCoords.TryGetValue(new Point(X, Y), out var value))
		{
			return true;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < value.maxItems; i++)
		{
			if (value.item[i] != null && value.item[i].type > 0 && value.item[i].stack > 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		int num = value.index;
		RemoveChest(num);
		if (Main.player[Main.myPlayer].chest == num)
		{
			Main.player[Main.myPlayer].chest = -1;
		}
		return true;
	}

	{

			return;

				_ = 0;
```

````
```ts
/** Chest.AddItemToShop（Chest.cs:651-670）会话投影：先扣回购记（Remove），记满覆盖
 *  不上架；残量克隆进 shopBuybackStock（buyOnce 货架）首空位（≤39） */
private shelfAfterAddToShop(memoKey: string, vid: number, prefix: number, stack: number): void {
  const memoBefore = this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0;
  this.consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey, stack);
  const num = Math.min(memoBefore, stack);         // Remove 返回的已覆盖量（:652）
  if (num >= stack) return;                        // :653-656 全覆盖 → 不上架
  if (this.shopBuybackStock.length >= 39) return;  // :654 首空位扫到 38（40 格末位不收）
  this.shopBuybackStock.push({ vid, prefix, stack: stack - num });  // :658-663 clone buyOnce stack-num
}
```
````

```
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public class ItemShopSellbackHelper
{
	private class ItemMemo
	{
		public readonly int type;

		public readonly int prefix;

		public int stack;

		public ItemMemo(Item item)
		{
			type = item.type;
			prefix = item.prefix;
			stack = item.stack;
		}

		public bool Matches(Item item)
		{
			if (item.IsConsideredSameItemAsType(type))
			{
				return item.prefix == prefix;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	private List<ItemMemo> _memos = new List<ItemMemo>();

	public void Add(Item item)
	{
		ItemMemo itemMemo = _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item));
		if (itemMemo != null)
		{
			itemMemo.stack += item.stack;
		}
		else
		{
			_memos.Add(new ItemMemo(item));
		}
	}

	public void Clear()
	{
		_memos.Clear();
	}

	public int GetAmount(Item item)
	{
		return _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item))?.stack ?? 0;
	}

	public int Remove(Item item)
	{
		ItemMemo itemMemo = _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item));
		if (itemMemo == null)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int stack = itemMemo.stack;
		itemMemo.stack -= item.stack;
		if (itemMemo.stack <= 0)
		{
			_memos.Remove(itemMemo);
			return stack;
		}
		return stack - itemMemo.stack;
	}
}

```

````
```ts
/** 商店购买（ItemSlot.HandleShopSlot ItemSlot.cs:1870-1931：左键/右键买入，
 *  :1894 GetItemExpectedPrice → :1895 BuyItem → :1910-1923 入光标/记账 →
 *  :1925-1928 buyOnce 减库存）。held = 光标持有物（同型 CanStack 才可买叠到光标，
 *  异型整链无动作 PickItemMovementAction case 15 :1427-1438 result -1） */
npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number, buybackIndex?: number, held?: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }): boolean {
  const bb = buybackIndex === undefined ? undefined : this.shopBuybackStock[buybackIndex];
  if (buybackIndex !== undefined && (!bb || bb.vid !== vanillaId)) return false;
  const prefix = bb ? bb.prefix : 0;
  // 常规货价/回购货架价（Player.GetItemExpectedPrice Player.cs:34907-34946）
  let price: number;
  if (bb) {
    // buyOnce 分支：:34930 round(value/pa) → :34940 ÷5 → :34941-34944 min1；
    // 折扣卡不参与（:34920 discount 门要求 !buyOnce）
    price = expectedPrices(itemValueWithPrefix(bb.vid, bb.prefix),
      { buyOnce: true, priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul }).buying;
  } else {
    const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];
    const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);
    if (!entry) return false;
    // 常规买价：:34921 折扣 (int)(×0.8f) 截断 → :34935 ×pa Math.Round（银行家）。
    // ★无 min-1 钳制——原版收费侧 calcForBuying 原值进 BuyItem（:34948 CoinsCount
    //  < price 才拒），仅显示侧有 min1（Main.cs:20341-20343）
    price = expectedPrices(entry[1], {
      priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,
      discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,
    }).buying;
  }
  // 光标持有门（:1878 !Item.CanStack 早退——type+prefix 全等 Item.cs:49446-4954；
  // :1890-1893 mouseItem 满叠停买；异型 = case 15 result -1 整链无动作）
  if (held) {
    const hd = ITEM_DEFS[held.id];
    const heldVid = hd ? (hd.vid ?? vanillaIdOfItemKey(hd.key)) : -1;
    if (heldVid !== vanillaId || (held.prefix ?? 0) !== prefix) return false;
    if (held.stack >= (hd?.maxStack ?? 1)) return false;
  }
  if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {
    this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
    this.openNpcShop();
    return false;
  }
  const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];
  if (held) held.stack++;                     // :1910-1923 买叠到光标（prefix 已全等）
  else if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1, prefix || undefined);
  if (!bb) {
    // 店内回购记（ItemSlot.cs:1919-1922：!buyOnce && shopSpecialCurrency==-1 → Add）
    const memoKey = `${vanillaId}:0`;
    this.shopSellbackMemo.set(memoKey, (this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0) + 1);
  } else if (--bb.stack <= 0) {
    this.shopBuybackStock.splice(buybackIndex!, 1);   // :1925-1928 归零下架
  }
  this.sfx.play('coin');
  this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  this.openNpcShop();
  return true;
}
```

npcShopSell rewrite with shelf + prefix:

```ts
npcShopSell(slot: number): boolean {
  ... same gates ...
  const quote = computeSellValue(vid, it.stack, {
    prefix: it.prefix ?? 0,                        // 词缀 value 缩放（Item.cs:596-597）
    priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,
    discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,
    sellbackAmount: this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0,
  });
  if (!quote.sellable) {
    // 白送分支：AddItemToShop（含回购记 Remove + 残量上架）+ TurnToAir + 音效 7
    this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, it.prefix ?? 0, it.stack);
    inv.slots[slot] = null;
    this.sfx.play('pickup');
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();
    return true;
  }
  const before = this.coinsOwned();
  inv.slots[slot] = null;
  this.gainCopper(quote.copper);
  if (this.coinsOwned() - before !== quote.copper) {
    // 装不下回滚：SellItem 内部失败 → AddItemToShop 不执行（ItemSlot.cs:783 门）
    const gained = this.coinsOwned() - before;
    if (gained > 0) this.spendCopper(gained);
    inv.slots[slot] = it;
    return true;
  }
  this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, it.prefix ?? 0, it.stack);   // chest.AddItemToShop（:785）
  this.sfx.play('coin');
  this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();
  return true;
}

/** Chest.AddItemToShop（Chest.cs:651-670）1:1 会话投影：回购记 Remove（:652，
 *  ItemShopSellbackHelper.cs:52-63 min 语义）→ 全覆盖不上架（:653-656）→ 残量
 *  克隆进 buyOnce 货架（:658-663 stack-=num；:654 首空位扫到 38 → 上限 39 条） */
private addItemToShopShelf(memoKey: string, vid: number, prefix: number, stack: number): void {
  const memoBefore = this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0;
  this.consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey, stack);
  const shelf = buybackShelfStack(stack, memoBefore);
  if (shelf > 0 && this.shopBuybackStock.length < 39) {
    this.shopBuybackStock.push({ vid, prefix, stack: shelf });
  }
}
```

The old separate `this.consumeSellbackMemo(...)` calls get replaced. But careful — in the give-away branch, old code consumed memo then cleared. New: addItemToShopShelf consumes + shelves. ✓

npcShopSellHeld:

```ts
/** 光标持有物整叠卖出（ItemSlot.LeftClick case 4 ItemSlot.cs:1110-1129：
 *  PickItemMovementAction case 15 :1435-1438 空商店格+非钱币 mouseItem → 4）。
 *  返回 true = 已售出/白送（调用方清光标）；false = 不动作或币装不下（光标保留） */
npcShopSellHeld(held: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }): boolean {
  if (!this.npcShopOpen) return false;
  const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(held.id);
  if (vid < 0) return false;
  if (!canSellFromShopUi(vid)) return false;      // 钱币 71-74（:1435 门）无动作
  const prefix = held.prefix ?? 0;
  const memoKey = `${vid}:${prefix}`;
  const quote = computeSellValue(vid, held.stack, {
    prefix, priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,
    discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,
    sellbackAmount: this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0,
  });
  if (!quote.sellable) {
    this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, prefix, held.stack);  // :1121-1122 白送入店
    this.sfx.play('pickup');                                     // :1124 音效 7
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();
    return true;
  }
  const before = this.coinsOwned();
  this.gainCopper(quote.copper);
  if (this.coinsOwned() - before !== quote.copper) {
    const gained = this.coinsOwned() - before;
    if (gained > 0) this.spendCopper(gained);
    return false;               // SellItem false → mouseItem 原样保留（:1113 门）
  }
  this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, prefix, held.stack);     // :1115 AddItemToShop
  this.sfx.play('coin');                                          // :1117 音效 18
  this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();
  return true;                                                    // :1116 SetDefaults(0)
}
```
````

```json
 "old_string": "  /** 商店购买:扣币 + 入包 + 刷新商店面板(可购态/余额) */\n  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number): void {\n    const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];\n    const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);\n    if (!entry) return;\n    // 折扣卡（discountAvailable：PriceAdjustment 0.8，Main.cs:41028 同源）× 快乐度乘子\n    const price = Math.max(1, Math.round(entry[1] * (this.player.equipStats.discount ? 0.8 : 1) * this.shopHappinessMul));\n    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {\n      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));\n      this.openNpcShop();\n      return;\n    }\n    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];\n    if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1);\n    // 店内回购记（ItemSlot.cs:1919-1922：!buyOnce && shopSpecialCurrency==-1 → Add；\n    // 本仓商店条目两者恒假/无自定义币种 → 无条件记。prefix 恒 0：购买链不带词缀）\n    const memoKey = `${vanillaId}:0`;\n    this.shopSellbackMemo.set(memoKey, (this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0) + 1);\n    this.sfx.play('coin');\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    this.openNpcShop();\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** 商店购买（ItemSlot.HandleShopSlot ItemSlot.cs:1870-1931）：左键买入 =\n   *  :1894 GetItemExpectedPrice（买价 calcForBuying）→ :1895 BuyItem（扣币）→\n   *  :1910-1923 入光标/背包 + 记回购 → :1925-1928 buyOnce 减库存归零下架。\n   *  buybackIndex = 卖回货架条目下标（缺省 = 常规货）；held = 光标持有物\n   *  （PickItemMovementAction case 15 ItemSlot.cs:1426-1439：空手→3 买入；同型\n   *  CanStack（Item.cs:49446-4954 type+prefix 全等）且未满叠（:1890-1893）→ 3 买入\n   *  叠到光标；异型 → result -1 整链无动作）。返回是否成交 */\n  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number, buybackIndex?: number,\n    held?: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }): boolean {\n    const bb = buybackIndex === undefined ? undefined : this.shopBuybackStock[buybackIndex];\n    if (buybackIndex !== undefined && (!bb || bb.vid !== vanillaId)) return false;\n    const prefix = bb ? bb.prefix : 0;\n    // 光标持有门（:1878 !CanStack 早退 + :1890-1893 满叠停买）\n    if (held) {\n      const hd = ITEM_DEFS[held.id];\n      if (!hd || vanillaIdOfItem(held.id) !== vanillaId) return false;\n      if ((held.prefix ?? 0) !== prefix) return false;\n      if (held.stack >= (hd.maxStack ?? 1)) return false;\n    }\n    let price: number;\n    if (bb) {\n      // buyOnce 买回价（Player.cs:34930 round(value/pa) → :34940 ÷5 → :34941-34944\n      // min1；折扣卡不参与——:34919-34921 的 discount 门要求 !buyOnce）；\n      // value 含词缀缩放（卖出克隆带的 scaled value，Item.cs:596-597）\n      price = expectedPrices(itemValueWithPrefix(bb.vid, bb.prefix),\n        { buyOnce: true, priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul }).buying;\n    } else {\n      const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];\n      const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);\n      if (!entry) return false;\n      // 常规买价 1:1（Player.GetItemExpectedPrice Player.cs:34916-34935）：\n      //   :34921 折扣 (int)(calcForBuying × 0.8f) ——(int) 向零截断，先截后乘\n      //   :34935 calcForBuying = (int)Math.Round(× PriceAdjustment) ——银行家舍入\n      //   ★无 min-1 钳制：calcForBuying 原值直接进 BuyItem（:34948 CoinsCount <\n      //     price 才拒付）；仅显示侧有 min1（Main.cs:20341-20343）\n      price = expectedPrices(entry[1], {\n        priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,\n        discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,\n      }).buying;\n    }\n    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {\n      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));\n      this.openNpcShop();\n      return false;\n    }\n    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];\n    if (held) held.stack++;   // :1910-1923 买叠到光标（CanStack 门已保证 prefix 全等）\n    else if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) {\n      this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1, prefix || undefined);  // :1912-1916 货架词缀随行\n    }\n    if (bb) {\n      // buyOnce 不进回购记（:1919-1922 !buyOnce 门）；减库存归零下架（:1925-1928）\n      if (--bb.stack <= 0) this.shopBuybackStock.splice(buybackIndex!, 1);\n    } else {\n      // 店内回购记（ItemSlot.cs:1919-1922：!buyOnce && shopSpecialCurrency==-1 → Add；\n      // 本仓常规商店条目两者恒假/无自定义币种 → 无条件记。prefix 恒 0：货架购买不带词缀）\n      const memoKey = `${vanillaId}:0`;\n      this.shopSellbackMemo.set(memoKey, (this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0) + 1);\n    }\n    this.sfx.play('coin');    // :1899-1908 value>0 → 18 Coins（本仓表无 value-0 货）\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    this.openNpcShop();\n    return true;\n  }"

0, it.stack);  // chest.AddItemToShop（:785）\n    this.sfx.play('coin');                          // SoundID 18 = Coins（:788）\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();         // 刷新面板余额（同 npcShopBuy）\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 光标持有物整叠卖出（ItemSlot.LeftClick case 4，ItemSlot.cs:1110-1129）：\n   *  触发门 = PickItemMovementAction case 15（:1435-1438）——mouseItem 非空、点中\n   *  的商店格为空、type 非钱币 71-74 → result 4。结算：\n   *  :1113 SellItem(mouseItem) 成功 → :1115 AddItemToShop + :1116 mouseItem 清空 +\n   *  :1117 音效 18；value==0 → :1120-1126 白送入店 + 音效 7。\n   *  返回 true = 已售出/白送（调用方清光标）；false = 无动作/币装不下（光标原样） */\n  npcShopSellHeld(held: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }): boolean {\n    if (!this.npcShopOpen) return false;\n    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(held.id);\n    if (vid < 0) return false;\n    if (!canSellFromShopUi(vid)) return false;      // 钱币 71-74 → result -1（:1435）\n    const prefix = held.prefix ?? 0;\n    const memoKey = `${vid}:${prefix}`;\n    const quote = computeSellValue(vid, held.stack, {\n      prefix,\n      priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,\n      discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,\n      sellbackAmount: this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0, // :34739 GetAmount\n    });\n    if (!quote.sellable) {\n      this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, prefix, held.stack);  // :1122 白送入店\n      this.sfx.play('pickup');                                    // :1124 音效 7\n      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n      if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();\n      return true;                                                // :1123 mouseItem 清空\n    }\n    const before = this.coinsOwned();\n    this.gainCopper(quote.copper);                  // :34745-34868 铂/金/银/铜换装\n    if (this.coinsOwned() - before !== quote.copper) {\n      // 装不下 → SellItem false → mouseItem 原样保留（:1113 门，货也不回架）\n      const gained = this.coinsOwned() - before;\n      if (gained > 0) this.spendCopper(gained);\n      return false;\n    }\n    this.addItemToShopShelf(memoKey, vid, prefix, held.stack);     // :1115 AddItemToShop\n    this.sfx.play('coin');                                          // :1117 音效 18\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();\n    return true;                                                    // :1116 SetDefaults(0)\n  }\n\n  /** Chest.AddItemToShop（Chest.cs:651-670）1:1 会话投影：先扣回购记（:652 Remove，\n   *  ItemShopSellbackHelper.cs:52-63 实扣 = min(记账, stack)）→ 记账全覆盖不上架\n   *  （:653-656）→ 残量克隆进 buyOnce 货架（:658-663 buyOnce=true、favorited=false、\n   *  stack-=num；:654 首空位扫 0-38 → 上限 39 条） */\n  private addItemToShopShelf(memoKey: string, vid: number, prefix: number, stack: number): void {\n    const memoBefore = this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0;\n    this.consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey, stack);\n    const shelf = buybackShelfStack(stack, memoBefore);\n    if (shelf > 0 && this.shopBuybackStock.length < 39) {\n      this.shopBuybackStock.push({ vid, prefix, stack: shelf });\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 词缀显示名（Lang.GetPrefixedItemName 渲染侧：\"Prefix.{ConstName} + 物品名\"，\n   *  与 UI.prefixDisplayName 同式）——卖回货架条目名带词缀 */\n  private static prefixNameOf(prefix: number): string {\n    const key = PREFIX_NAMES[String(prefix)];\n    if (!key) return '';\n    const t = Lang.text(`Prefix.${key}`);\n    return t && t !== `Prefix.${key}` ? `${t} ` : `${key} `;\n  }"
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "  it('纯函数边界：settings 对象不被就地改写（无共享可变状态，存档/会话无关）', () => {\n    const s = { priceAdjustment: 1.12, discountAvailable: true, sellbackAmount: 2 };\n    const snapshot = { ...s };\n    computeSellValue(39, 5, s);\n    expect(s).toEqual(snapshot);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 收尾批（2026-08-14）：买入侧截断链去近似（Player.GetItemExpectedPrice\n// buying 分支）/ 词缀 value 缩放（Item.Prefix）/ 光标持有出售（ItemSlot.LeftClick\n// case 4）/ 卖出回 buyOnce 货架（Chest.AddItemToShop）。\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe('买入侧价格链 1:1（Player.cs:34916-34935）', () => {\n  it('折扣 (int) 截断先于快乐度乘区（:34921→:34935 顺序）：101 价折扣+1.5 → 80×1.5=120（旧近似 round(101×0.8×1.5)=121 会多收 1 铜）', () => {\n    // :34921 (int)(101 × 0.8f) = (int)80.8000012 = 80；:34935 round(80 × 1.5f) = 120\n    expect(expectedPrices(101, { discountAvailable: true, priceAdjustment: 1.5 }).buying).toBe(120);\n    expect(Math.max(1, Math.round(101 * 0.8 * 1.5))).toBe(121);  // 对照组：旧近似确实错\n  });\n\n  it('买价 ×PriceAdjustment 银行家舍入（:34935 Math.Round）：15×1.5 = 22.5 → 22（Math.round 给 23）', () => {\n    expect(expectedPrices(15, { priceAdjustment: 1.5 }).buying).toBe(22);\n    expect(Math.round(15 * 1.5)).toBe(23);\n  });\n\n  it('买价无 min-1 钳制：折扣把 1 价砍到 (int)0.8→0 → 实收 0（min1 只在显示侧 Main.cs:20341-20343 与 buyOnce :34941-34944）', () => {\n    expect(expectedPrices(1, { discountAvailable: true }).buying).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('buyOnce 买回价不吃折扣（:34919-34921 门要求 !buyOnce）：100 价 1.5 快乐度 → round(100/1.5)=67 → ÷5 = 13', () => {\n    const p = expectedPrices(100, { buyOnce: true, priceAdjustment: 1.5, discountAvailable: true });\n    expect(p.buying).toBe(13);   // 67/5 = 13.4 → floor 13\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('词缀 value 缩放（Item.cs:596-597 num2 *= num2 平方乘区）', () => {\n  it('白板（prefix 0）恒走原值：39 号 = 100', () => {\n    expect(itemValueWithPrefix(39, 0)).toBe(100);\n    expect(itemValueWithPrefix(39)).toBe(100);\n  });\n\n  it('83 号词缀（dmg1.15/kb1.15/spd0.9/mcst0.9/crt5）：乘区 1.7602…平方 3.098…×100 → (int) = 309', () => {\n    expect(itemValueWithPrefix(39, 83)).toBe(309);\n  });\n\n  it('value 0 族缩放后仍 0：泥土 2 号带词缀照旧不可卖', () => {\n    expect(itemValueWithPrefix(2, 83)).toBe(0);\n    expect(computeSellValue(2, 1, { prefix: 83 }).sellable).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('词缀卖价走缩放值（GetStoreValue=缩放后 value）：39 号×83 词缀单件 = floor(309/5) = 61 铜', () => {\n    expect(computeSellValue(39, 1, { prefix: 83 }).perUnit).toBe(61);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('光标持有物出售（ItemSlot.LeftClick case 4 :1110-1129 / PickItemMovementAction case 15 :1426-1439）', () => {\n  it('钱币 71-74 持有点空商店格不动作（:1435 门）：canSellFromShopUi 全拒', () => {\n    for (const vid of [71, 72, 73, 74]) expect(canSellFromShopUi(vid)).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('整叠结算与格内同链：39 号持 7 件、回购记 2 件 = 20×7 + (100-20)×2 = 300 铜', () => {\n    expect(computeSellValue(39, 7, { sellbackAmount: 2 }).copper).toBe(300);\n  });\n\n  it('value 0 持有物 = 白送入店分支（:1120-1126 SellItem false && value==0）', () => {\n    const q = computeSellValue(9, 3);   // 木板 value 0\n    expect(q.sellable).toBe(false);\n    expect(q.copper).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('卖出回 buyOnce 货架（Chest.AddItemToShop Chest.cs:651-670）', () => {\n  it('记账全覆盖不上架（:653-656 num >= stack → return）', () => {\n    expect(buybackShelfStack(5, 5)).toBe(0);\n    expect(buybackShelfStack(5, 9)).toBe(0);   // 记 9 卖 5：实扣 min(9,5)=5 全覆盖\n  });\n\n  it('残量上架 stack-num（:663）：记 2 卖 5 → 上架 3；零记账卖 5 → 上架 5', () => {\n    expect(buybackShelfStack(5, 2)).toBe(3);\n    expect(buybackShelfStack(5, 0)).toBe(5);\n  });\n\n  it('买回再上架的对称价：value 100 白板卖 61 价词缀 83 → 回架价 = buyOnce(309) ÷5 = 61', () => {\n    // 卖出得 floor(309/5)=61/件；回架买回价 round(309/1)/5 = 61 —— 原价买回\n    expect(expectedPrices(itemValueWithPrefix(39, 83), { buyOnce: true }).buying).toBe(61);\n  });\n});"
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "  private installDragListeners() {\n    // 点击面板外的世界区域：光标持有物直接丢出（原版行为）\n    document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {\n      if (!this.heldStack) return;\n      const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;\n      if (t?.closest?.('.sw-slot') || t?.closest?.('.sw-panel') || t?.closest?.('.sw-btn')) return;\n      // NPC 商店面板（ItemSlot.Handle context 15 Main.cs:41279-41282 商店格区）：\n      //  · 行/按钮 → 各自 click 链（行 = 有货商店格；光标持有时 Game.npcShopBuy 内\n      //    的 CanStack/满叠门裁决，异型整链无动作 PickItemMovementAction :1427-1438）\n      //  · 空白区 = 空商店格 → 光标持有物整叠卖出（PickItemMovementAction case 15\n      //    :1435-1438 → LeftClick case 4 :1110-1129 SellItem(mouseItem)），\n      //    不丢世界、不放置\n      //  · NPC 对话/心情浮层也是 UI（mouseInterface），点它不丢世界\n      if (t?.closest?.('.sw-npc-shop')) {\n        if (t.closest('.sw-shop-item') || t.closest('button')) return;\n        if (this.game?.npcShopSellHeld(this.heldStack)) this.heldStack = null;  // :1116 SetDefaults(0)\n        this.refreshAll();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (t?.closest?.('.sw-npc-dialog') || t?.closest?.('.sw-happy-panel')) return;\n      this.throwHeldToWorld();\n      this.refreshAll();\n    });"
}
```

```
  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number, buybackIndex?: number,
    held?: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }): boolean {
    const bb = buybackIndex === undefined ? undefined : this.shopBuybackStock[buybackIndex];
    if (buybackIndex !== undefined && (!bb || bb.vid !== vanillaId)) return false;
    const prefix = bb ? bb.prefix : 0;
    // 光标持有门（:1878 !CanStack 早退 + :1890-1893 满叠停买）
    if (held) {
      const hd = ITEM_DEFS[held.id];
      if (!hd || vanillaIdOfItem(held.id) !== vanillaId) return false;
      if ((held.prefix ?? 0) !== prefix) return false;
      if (held.stack >= (hd.maxStack ?? 1)) return false;
    }
    let price: number;
    if (bb) {
      // buyOnce 买回价（Player.cs:34930 round(value/pa) → :34940 ÷5 → :34941-34944
      // min1；折扣卡不参与——:34919-34921 的 discount 门要求 !buyOnce）；
      // value 含词缀缩放（卖出克隆带的 scaled value，Item.cs:596-597）
      price = expectedPrices(itemValueWithPrefix(bb.vid, bb.prefix),
        { buyOnce: true, priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul }).buying;
    } else {
      const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];
      const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);
      if (!entry) return false;
      // 常规买价 1:1（Player.GetItemExpectedPrice Player.cs:34916-34935）：
      //   :34921 折扣 (int)(calcForBuying × 0.8f) ——(int) 向零截断，先截后乘
      //   :34935 calcForBuying = (int)Math.Round(× PriceAdjustment) ——银行家舍入
      //   ★无 min-1 钳制：calcForBuying 原值直接进 BuyItem（:34948 CoinsCount <
      //     price 才拒付）；仅显示侧有 min1（Main.cs:20341-20343）
      price = expectedPrices(entry[1], {
        priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,
        discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount,
      }).buying;
    }
    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
      this.openNpcShop();
      return false;
    }
    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];
    if (held) held.stack++;   // :1910-1923 买叠到光标（CanStack 门已保证 prefix 全等）
    else if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) {
      this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1, prefix || undefined);  // :1912-1916 货架词缀随行
    }
    if (bb) {
      // buyOnce 不进回购记（:1919-1922 !buyOnce 门）；减库存归零下架（:1925-1928）
      if (--bb.stack <= 0) this.shopBuybackStock.splice(buybackIndex!, 1);
    } else {
      // 店内回购记（ItemSlot.cs:1919-1922：!buyOnce && shopSpecialCurrency==-1 → Add；
      // 本仓常规商店条目两者恒假/无自定义币种 → 无条件记。prefix 恒 0：货架购买不带词缀）
      const memoKey = `${vanillaId}:0`;
      this.shopSellbackMemo.set(memoKey, (this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    this.sfx.play('coin');    // :1899-1908 value>0 → 18 Coins（本仓表无 value-0 货）
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    this.openNpcShop();
    return true;
  }
```

```
## 原版语义结论（行号均为 Terarria1456 反编译源码）

**项1 光标持有物点格（以源码为准）**
- 商店格 = context 15，`ItemSlot.PickItemMovementAction` case 15（ItemSlot.cs:1426-1439）：光标持有非钱币物品点**空商店格** → result 4 → `LeftClick` case 4（:1110-1129）`player.SellItem(Main.mouseItem)` **整叠卖出**，成功后 AddItemToShop + mouseItem 清空 + 音效 18；value==0 白送入店（:1120-1126，音效 7）；币装不下 → SellItem false → **光标原样保留**。钱币 71-74 点空格无动作（:1435 门）。
- 点**有货商店格**：同型（CanStack = type+prefix 全等，Item.cs:49446-4954）且未满叠 → 买入叠到光标（HandleShopSlot :1890-1923）；**异型 → result -1 整链无动作**。
- 点**玩家格**（context 0）= 正常放置/交换（case 0 → result 0），**不卖**——本仓 placeHeld 原本就正确，未动。

- :34921 折扣卡 `(int)(calcForBuying × 0.8f)` **(int) 向零截断**（先截后乘）；:34935 `calcForBuying = (int)Math.Round(× PriceAdjustment)` **银行家舍入**；**收费无 min-1 钳制**（BuyItem :34948 只做 CoinsCount < price 拒付），min-1 仅在显示侧（Main.cs:20341-20343）与 buyOnce（:34941-34944）。

**项3 卖出回货架（与任务前提不同，以源码为准）**
- `Chest.AddItemToShop`（Chest.cs:651-670）：卖出/白送物品**确实回货架**——先扣回购记（:652 Remove），记账全覆盖则不上架（:653-656），残量克隆进 item[0-39] 首空位、**buyOnce=true**、stack-=记账量（:658-663）。买回价 = buyOnce 分支（:34930 ÷pa round → :34940 ÷5 → min1；折扣不参与 :34920 门），buyOnce 购买不进回购记（HandleShopSlot :1919-1922）、减库存归零下架（:1925-1928）。货架是会话态：仅 Main.OpenShop→SetupShop（Main.cs:39895-39903）重建。**发现偏差已修**：本仓原缺 buyOnce 货架。
```
